[EVENT: SYR122508]
Date: 2023-12-09
Country: Syria | Location: Dargir - Deir al Kabir
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Hamza Division - Aleppo [Rebel group]
Actor2: Civilians (Syria) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: SOHR [Other]

DESCRIPTION:
Around 9 December 2023 (week of), Hamza Division fighters physically beat and tortured a civilian then detained him after robbing his home in Dargir - Deir al Kabir in rural northern Aleppo, seizing his vehicle and cell phones. The victims' whereabouts remain unknown.

[EVENT: SYR122536]
Date: 2023-12-09
Country: Syria | Location: Nawa
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Unidentified Armed Group (Syria) [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Syria) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Dar'a 24; SOHR [Other-Subnational]

DESCRIPTION:
Around 9 December 2023 (week of), an unidentified armed group poisoned a civilian at a luncheon in Nawa city in rural Dara who is accused of being a regime collaborator, killing him several days later in a hospital in Damascus city. The victim was a former opposition rebel member who joined the regime-affiliated military intelligence after signing a reconciliation agreement with the regime before finally returning to civilian life. Conflicting reports suggest the victim may have succumbed to illness from sustained shrapnel wounds instead of poisoning. 1 fatality.

---
